# Lu — Canonical development ledger

### LU-IPC-GPU-01D-R2 — installed NVP broker integration (2026-11-21, PR #244)

**Protected NVP worker-stop custody correction (same R2 task):** The public desktop cannot open the SYSTEM/Administrators-only NVP Job-empty receipt or its independently installed P-256 signer pin. The production GPU-release path now obtains the original recovered lease scope from Lu Core's durable journal and sends it over the already CNG-signed, nonce-challenged Core→SCM control pipe. The SCM verifies the exact lease-named receipt against the protected NVP signer pin *under unimpersonated SYSTEM*, returning a bounded success response only for a correct signature, live original service generation and matching original user/worker scope. No user-mode key, receipt file read, untrusted `all clear`, filesystem selector or substitute signer is admitted. Final reload additionally requires original Core authority, measured VRAM availability and the exact model/MMProj restoration lifecycle. Windows native refusal tests cover stale SCM, replay, scope/path forgery, malformed envelope and non-SYSTEM protected evidence access. This is SOURCE only pending new Windows exact-SHA acceptance and physical installed NVP custody receipts.\n\n

**Current native desktop integration (source-only):** `lu-desktop::nvp_gpu_desktop` now provides a private, non-WebView installed-NVP admission entrypoint. It binds an exact CURRENT persisted ANSF USER turn (not recalled/model/tool text), genuine native registered `nvp.film` READ/EXECUTE descriptors, original call fingerprint/session/epoch, independent live signed SCM witness and host-only native OS consent dialog. The desktop owns a revocable single active film grant; P0 global Stop revokes that exact turn and signals cancellation. The existing Core on-demand Protobuf listener receives the desktop's original `ConversationAuthority` and already-recovered GPU lease and async WAL. On failed consent, Stop, altered original turn, missing registered NVP module or missing SCM proof, the operation does **not** grant GPU or restore Lu's model. The Windows desktop uses the locked, existing Tokio runtime for cancellable native async admission. **The installed producer/call site is not present yet:** the interface cannot be called from a WebView or ordinary model output, and it is deliberately not auto-started at boot. This means source bridge implementation is not genuine installed interoperability. Native Windows source checks and physical acceptance are pending the next exact SHA.



**State: SOURCE BRIDGE IMPLEMENTED; GENUINE INSTALLED/DESKTOP INTEROPERABILITY AND PHYSICAL RADEON ACCEPTANCE OPEN.** Existing draft PR #244 (the only Core IPC integration branch) completed its exact Windows/Linux native qualification at `554bb6c99cd0449c52883b3a3282679b93aa772f` in [CI #44949809560](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/44949809560). It includes a genuinely SYSTEM-only NVP HMAC verifier (no exported key), original-call/process/SCM-generation canonical scope, a bounded in-SCM revocable session registry, authenticated public-only installer pin handoff, a Core named-pipe NVP listener tied to `HostCoreTask` plus `ConversationAuthority` and exact READ/EXECUTE original-turn plan, a final-GRANTED broker recheck, and separate NVP signed Job-empty verification before model reload. Native Windows/Linux + negative source suites passed; this is **not** installed acceptance.

**Current corrective source:** native Core now obtains the NVP PID/birth/session/SID from the **held signed named-pipe process witness** immediately after Hello, instead of requiring a caller-selected PID. It adds hostile-process/credential-negative tests. The native Core listener also requires the exact currently registered `nvp.film` READ and EXECUTE descriptors. During asynchronous unload and final GPU `GRANTED`, the host rechecks the live registration and holds a short registry read fence together with the original-turn grant while committing the durable final lease; NVP unregistration/replacement or revocation cannot inherit an earlier approval. The registry lock is never held across host-policy provider calls, SCM I/O, GPU probes, or GGUF unload. Native registry cross-platform regression tests are added, pending new exact-source qualification. Exact latest-SHA native requalification is required after final source-lock publication; previous CI does not qualify a newer head. Source also retains the scoped on-demand listener without auto-starting it in DesktopState: a real installed film creator approval and NVP signed installation must provide an independently verified original USER turn and NVP client proof first. There is no fabricated film grant, installed NVP SCM issuer, hardware result or release.

**Next existing task, no new task ID:** finish **LU-IPC-GPU-01D-R2**—bind the desktop's real reviewed film operation to the on-demand Core listener, verify enrolled Core↔NVP credential/interoperability and signed native LTX stop receipts, then perform genuine physical Radeon R9700 GGUF→LTX→GGUF unload/release/reload, cancellation/revocation/restart and uncertain-stop acceptance. NVP owns its client and worker issuer; Lu Core owns the host, broker, GPU lease and source contract. See [IPC handoff contract](LU_IPC_GPU_01C_DURABLE_DESKTOP_NVP_20261121.md), [PR #244](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/pull/244), and Core dependency [issue #250](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/issues/250). Issue #245 Core Health remains a separate open dependency; do not relabel it as GPU-01D work. Keep single CI cohort, draft PR and default-deny physical release.

#### Design and measurement guardrails

Use [canonical execution-layer philosophy](LU_AI_EXECUTION_LAYER_PHILOSOPHY_20261107.md): **C_practical = f(K,I,A,F)**; K = knowledge/reasoning, I = real environmental interfaces, A = user-governed authorized resource/effect, F = feedback/independent verification/recovery. User defines purpose; effective knowledge includes source-bound memory. Treat this as conceptual, not a weighted score. Model does not grant itself authority. Present truthful capability availability even before authorization; grant exact action only after applicable policy and consent. Benchmark improvement against equivalent authorizations in tool-enabled comparison arms; model-only baseline cannot claim external effects. Acceptance requires correct independently witnessed outcomes, zero unauthorized effects and no regression of original controls; report observed efficiency/quality tradeoffs rather than asserting speedup.

**Next actionable task: CP-01 transactional attachment lifecycle, then group CP-02–04 and CP-05–06 as evidence-driven repairs; CP-07/08 may proceed alongside once contracts are pinned.** Complete CP-09 benchmark and CP-10 consolidated acceptance before calling any subsequent build ready. No runtime modifications or CI dispatch in this documentation checkpoint.



#### CP-01A — native admission transaction foundation (2026-11-16; staged candidate only)

Reconciled design baseline `0676d82a05068ab1fea54fffa209ab87d9cbe82b`, source commit [`e0f5e4bc`](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/commit/e0f5e4bc555d28585ce3642394eaca8a0fd5c19b). Ordinary generate_internal now orders queue publication after the host journal admission callback succeeds; conflicting reservations reject before the callback. Background continuation keeps its original admission ordering; the unbounded background history read is deliberately outside the scheduler mutex. Exact authenticated turn identity and original text are rechecked after queue wait, including identical-text turns. ANSF gains a transaction-aware artifact-link insert reused by the standalone API and a host-authenticated original-user event + reference batch API; a malformed second reference rolls back the original event, source entry, and links. CP-12A narrow instrumentation: existing host per-turn PERF receipt records queue publication boolean and admitted image count, and records failed queue publication before dispatch; no raw content or claimed UI/worker outcome. This does not complete original CP-12A causal tracing. Unit regression cases added for failed/conflicting admission and invalid/duplicate/cross-chat/reopened artifact references. No compiler, test, installed, real MMProj or physical result claimed; local Git DNS and Rust toolchain unavailable. The new artifact API is not yet connected to external byte staging or ordinary UI storage. No persisted Observe pixels, path grants, network egress, IDE/Kobolt or automatic CI dispatch. Remaining CP-01: one native draft/turn manifest, exact ordered immutable external bytes and digest readback, per-conversation ownership (currently global registry), no lost/overwritten draft on error/cancel, new-chat transition, separate human instruction and attachment data, saved chips/reload, actual backend/projector path and real one/four-image unknown-pixel R9700 test. Derived catalog is separate from the book; reconcile any post-book/pre-queue failure without claiming cross-store atomicity. Do not merge or declare CP-01/CP-12A accepted until grouped source and behavioral verification.


#### CP-01B — ordered host-owned image manifest and durable native custody (2026-11-17 UTC; staged candidate only)

Source baseline CP-01A `8b7da80e62c4b0abef8c7bc845a9c4c977643e99`; CP-01B source candidate [`037a20ae`](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/commit/037a20ae65627011ccd7a6b7ca1724b7a4e86b82). Supersedes CP-01A checkpoint's global image registry and unconnected artifact-reference findings for ordinary PNG/JPEG. Canonical per-book ANSF image manifest is ordered by host insertion sequence; draft rows bind to the exact original authenticated human turn, ordinary artifact links and visible-message projection in the same SQLite transaction before human queue publication. Four images, 8 MiB each, 16 MiB aggregate; stale/omitted/reordered/cross-chat refs rejected; saved rows immutable and stale release cannot delete original bytes. Random host handles separate ownership from Core's content identity. Ordinary bytes use a host-private handle-relative create-new, file flush, Linux directory sync, digest/decode readback store outside SQLite. Draft and saved reads require exact chat and visible original; later model dispatch rechecks manifest+artifact-link identity and full bytes. Observe metadata is marked local-only and its pixels remain memory-only; ordinary read/serialization refuses Observe, expired observations explicitly unavailable, no automatic pixel persistence or network grant. Existing scoped OCR, image revision, picker and paste/drop paths use the same custody. Derived catalog failure after a successful original-image book commit is not returned as rejected admission; sanitized existing PERF receipt marks reconciliation needed. Existing non-image and background admission retained. Bounded orphan scan (4,096 entries) deletes only when a known book proves absence; unknown/unavailable objects retained, large scans incomplete. Windows inherited profile ACL and NTFS power-loss/reparse behavior still require native/physical verification.

Minimal renderer protocol change establishes a real native chat before attaching, scopes explicit release to origin, refreshes manifest membership, and removes unconditional post-Send release. **Not CP-01C completion:** optimistic clearing, exact retry/cancel restoration of original prompt/text files, saved chips/preview, same-chat publication acknowledgment and comprehensive renderer interleaving/reload tests still open; text/document attachment authority remains CP-02. Source-only checks: 21/21 scoped wiring assertions directly on fetched source; SQLite schema smoke passed insertion order, conversation scope, rollback, saved-row immutability and stale-release denial (not Rust runtime execution). Added six native unit cases across manifest and object store, three JS source-contract tests and updated legacy source assertions for the new ownership path; no Cargo/Node full suite run. Local checkout DNS and Rust toolchain unavailable. No real model, installed, native OS acceptance, CI dispatch, installer, main merge, IDE/Kobolt change or CP-12A completion. Next: **CP-01C**, then CP-01D actual MMProj backend, CP-01E causal tracing, CP-01F grouped verification and installed physical R9700 tests. Maintain CP-01 OPEN until evidence passes.


#### CP-01C — frontend transactional Send, cancellation and saved attachment recovery (2026-11-17 UTC; source candidate)

CP-01B baseline `3d3168e257933881e24d62627921d190d558f3f8`; CP-01C source candidate [`a3b561b2`](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/commit/a3b561b265ef316857745212fb23933539f20430). Ordinary image Send snapshots raw editable prompt, ordered opaque image references and existing bounded text-file draft before any composer cleanup. A native chat-bound correlation nonce maps to one deterministic generation ID and exact immutable original visible sequence; it authorizes neither tools nor filesystem access. Native preparation writes one bounded UI recovery snapshot per conversation on explicit Send (not a new model or ANSF source). Under the same attachment gate, native inference verifies the prepared exact prompt/manifest, and a refusal record prevents a delayed same-nonce request from consuming a retained draft. Native typed outcome distinguishes settled refusal, saved original, and transport uncertainty. The frontend clears only the exact confirmed submitted draft, not on a queue event, text match, failed model response or optimistic UI update. Uncertain Send offers explicit Check/retry same message or Keep draft without sending; a settled saved message is never automatically repeated. Saved original images survive queued/active cancellation and backend errors; user may restore the entire ordinary saved message into an empty draft using fresh object identities and the exact original text, then explicitly send again. No private Observe capture is restored/exported; expired draft observations require replacement. History window returns bounded ordered attachment metadata; saved preview is lazy and exact-chat+original-visible-sequence scoped. Ordinary drafts can be attached before loading a model, native preflight still requires compatible projector to send. New-conversation handoff, same-chat intake/Reader/remove serialization, background-independent sends, exact job cleanup and stale navigation/readback are guarded. Recovery reads a pending submitted snapshot/receipt on reload, never auto-invokes inference. General unsent prompt/file autosaving is outside this change; legacy document text remains embedded in submitted text pending CP-02 typed source separation (same-UI original file draft and pending Send snapshot preserve its data). Existing non-image/native-tool routes retain their authority and keep the composer until returned result instead of clearing optimistically; exact durable correlation is specific to image Send. Existing image success telemetry preserved.

Validation: five executable Node lifecycle tests passed locally; module Git blob and the five-test portion verified unchanged from the executed files, 12/12 focused source-contract tests evaluated directly on fetched candidate, four exact edited non-JSX region syntax checks passed. Four new native unit tests added (scope-bound correlation, refusal fence, atomic restore, unresolved snapshot lifecycle); not executed. Local Git DNS fails and Cargo absent; full JSX build, full Node suite, Rust tests, installed WebView event interleavings and cold-restart acceptance not established. No CI dispatched, no main merge, no IDE changes. CP-01C staged for grouped verification; CP-01 remains OPEN. Next: **CP-01D — actual GGUF/MMProj backend integration and demonstrated failure diagnostics**, then CP-01E integrated CP-12A causal tracing and CP-01F consolidated installed/physical acceptance (one and four unknown-pixel images with Qwen/R9700).


#### CP-01D — exact GGUF/MMProj backend delivery and demonstrated failure localization (2026-11-17 UTC; source candidate)

CP-01C baseline `a10a9ed59862dae37e0cd539609d9fcdf2eb261b`; CP-01D grouped source [`29e9869c`](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/commit/29e9869cf3de476bfebf0142bb5eac67a98ff8ad). Confirmed source gaps: image reservation validated a proposed frame but retained only the generation identity, so a later valid reordered/substituted frame could pass; the shared text run could borrow that identity. The bridge compared digest metadata without independently hashing actual pinned bytes. The image SSE path interpreted any nonnull finish reason as terminal, could stop before [DONE], and did not distinguish clipped, malformed/failed or whitespace-only output. Persistent backend unload on stream failure left a stale ready model/session in the supervisor. Ordinary Send now shares exact ANSF saved-manifest+full-byte readback with the native acceptance harness. Native supervisor binds the complete validated Protobuf image frame digest (including ordered image identities, prompt, request, token budget and history), refuses text/browser modality substitution, claims image dispatch once, and releases a rejected reservation. Bridge independently checks actual bytes; final production llama.cpp JSON, after host sampling/cache settings, independently decodes and compares every ordered original inline image bytes/MIME/hash, prompt and prior history before opening the socket. No external image URL or text fallback. Backend records upload completion only after actual complete local socket write; image-specific SSE requires [DONE], nonwhitespace delivered text and refuses terminal failure, truncation and token-limit success. Clipped partial text remains available for original journaling. Verified idle slot is separate from answer/transport receipt. An ejected native backend removes only the corresponding dead session while its original generation is claimed; no automatic restart. Existing text/tool transports preserved. Existing per-turn PERF ledger carries bounded host-derived image stage, safe failure code and actual transport count/bytes/boolean receipt (no source hashes, paths, text, pixels, keys or engine body), distinct from semantic pixel proof. Early refusals record before-queue/insufficient evidence; native wrapper reports safe user-readable reason, original images remain available. Image clock suffix cannot overrun a full admitted human prompt. No broad DX export or CP-12A completion.

Tests added: independently inspecting real HTTP socket fixture for four exact ordered original PNG byte arrays; refusal of reorder/omission/changed bytes/remote URLs; partial/backend/length/missing-DONE/whitespace failure fixtures; supervisor substitution and ejected-session regression. Existing missing-projector test now requires exact bounded failure code and no upload. New ignored opt-in desktop native test `actual_cp01_saved_one_and_four_images_reach_supervised_mmproj` requires independently expected engine/GGUF/MMProj SHA-256, starts real pinned PersistentLlamaBackend, uses production attachment store, ANSF original, foreground queue, saved readback and actual supervisor/bridge/HTTP; two unseen single-image colors and two independently ordered four-image permutations, exact actual words and transport/idle witnesses. No actual Tauri renderer in this harness; installed ordinary Send remains mandatory CP-01F. See `docs/LU_CP01_MULTIMODAL_DELIVERY_ACCEPTANCE.md` for commands and physical evidence. 11/11 focused JS source contracts executed directly against fetched source (5 new, 6 preserved). This environment has Node and Git but no Cargo, rustc, llama-server or working Git DNS; native compilation, new Rust tests, live server and physical model were NOT executed. No CI dispatch/merge/installer/IDE changes. CP-01D source repair staged, real inference verification remains OPEN. Next **CP-01E — integrated CP-12A causal diagnostic tracing**, then CP-01F exact-SHA consolidated Windows/Linux tests, real native model harness + installed Qwen3.8/R9700 one/four-image pixel/cancel/restart verification. Do not declare complete on source or synthetic socket evidence.

### Current priority — CORE-MERGE single-main convergence (November 13, 2026)

**Strategic execution-layer correction (owner recollection reaffirmed, 2026-11-13):** Preserve the November 7 [canonical AI Execution Layer philosophy](LU_AI_EXECUTION_LAYER_PHILOSOPHY_20261107.md) and [tool audit](LU_AI_EXECUTION_TOOL_REVIEW_20261107.md): **model intelligence + usable interfaces + user/host authority + real feedback**, not a growing count of domain-specific native tools. The Qwen document/spreadsheet experiment was illustrative evidence that effective task framing, selecting a fitting interface and recovering from an observed failure matter; it was **not** a general success benchmark. Reader/OCR, image utilities, browser, files, archive, typed execution and native tools already comprise sufficient source-level breadth for current Core consolidation. Future broad tool/module work is **DEFERRED BY DEFAULT**, even if an older branch contains absent files. A general JSON Schema utility, old environment-discovery command, second OCR fallback or second loop controller is **not** an automatic P0 development task: first show a user-task failure unhandled by the existing tool/execution stack, and only then consider a small audited primitive. Do not restore or reference the excluded legacy ANSF side adapter.

**Core convergence order remains C1→C3→C4→C5**, but the *functional emphasis* is the end-to-end **goal → relevant ANSF/task context → capability/prerequisite discovery → model plan and tool choice → exact current human/host grant → OS-supervised tool or app execution → independently observed result → verified completion or safe replan/recovery**. Favor exposing proven existing operations to the ordinary GGUF desktop workflow over building another image/Office/JSON micro-tool. Treat process/API/GUI execution as separately reviewed, limited effect surfaces (never ambient arbitrary shell, background permissions or model self-authorization). Evaluate on representative Qwen tasks with original artifacts, real tool-use choices, failure recovery and no invented success. Respect batch acceptance after a coherent source-integration group; do not start per-bug CI or a new branch/PR merely to pursue this correction. This strategic scope **supersedes** any historical next-action text below that prematurely ranks a new general-purpose validator or other domain tool above execution-layer consolidation.

**Authoritative execution backlog.** This section supersedes earlier historical tool-by-tool queue assumptions where functionality is already inherited in current Core source. The detailed, exact-head [Lu Core branch/PR inventory](LU_CORE_CONSOLIDATION_INVENTORY_20261113.md) is the source-graph receipt and must be reobserved if tips move. `main` is the canonical development index, not evidence that draft PRs have merged.

**Important source contract conflict:** First-party `lu-linux-cgroup-guard.rs::protected_ancestor` requires the current parent `cgroup.procs` be nonwritable by the nonroot worker UID to prevent ancestor escape. Existing `lu-win-worker-job/src/lib.rs::bind_worker_to_cgroup_before_exec` subsequently attempts a same-UID nonroot child `cgroup.procs` migration from exactly that protected parent to a new leaf. Linux cgroup-v2 permission rules require write permission to destination **and nearest common ancestor** `cgroup.procs`; this is the protected parent. Simply enabling `Delegate=yes` or setting the `lu-cgroup-v2-delegated` runner label cannot make both conditions hold safely. A fixed, independently root-owned SEC-06C broker must admit/migrate the worker before privilege drop (or equivalent kernel-proven restricted admission), while preserving the original Python destructive test and original Rust Git/restart test intact. ## 1. Status rules (never collapse these)

| Evidence level | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| **Designed** | A requirement or interface exists; no runnable implementation is asserted. |
| **Draft source** | Exact code exists on a named branch/PR but may not be present on `main`. |
| **Compiled/tested** | Specific CI checks passed on the exact head SHA; **list failing/skipped jobs too**. |
| **Desktop integrated** | The production Tauri host/UI can reach that implementation at an exact compiled revision; static registration is not sufficient. |
| **Model exercised** | Real local GGUF/model used the tool in a representative authorized task. |
| **Installed accepted** | Actual installed Windows build executed the owner-approved acceptance scenario with recorded binary/source identity. |
| **Released** | An immutable tagged/distributed release with traceable artifacts, separate from development acceptance. |

No branch status, passing isolated test, code comment, diagram, or prior chat can promote an item to another level. Record **what was tested, where, and at which exact SHA**. Mark unknown results unknown. A successful earlier alpha does not confer acceptance on later binaries.

### 2026-11-05 TOOL-F5 — first unified toolset build (current source of truth)

- **[Unified draft PR #34](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/pull/34)**, branch `lu/tools-01-05-unified-integration-20261105`, latest repair candidate `db3e55721de25b96328c78f10fd453dc000f6f91` (previous tested snapshot `a6c7153988644ccf6867b294ea17b14e61cabab1`). Based on existing #24 development branch; imported 30 unchanged Reader/background/ZIP source files from development PRs #27 and #15 by blob SHA, then reconciled Core Protobuf and modules, Rust/Windows worker ABI, 3 conflicting Tauri/React/chat-journal files, and added one combined native/tool UI CI matrix.
- **Confirmed at this exact head**: [Lu Desktop native-core tests](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42092433475/job/127473685280) **passed**; [source identity](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42092433475/job/127473684756) **passed**; [full Core+desktop Cargo.lock graph audit](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42092433718/job/127473692113) **passed**. These verify unified source and native Core compilation/tests, **not** an installed release.
- **Integration additions:** `ModelToolCatalog::absorb_registered` atomically verifies independently registered native tool domains against the actual registry; combined Reader + read-only ZIP + background status/receipt allowlist regression at `src-tauri/lu-core/tests/unified_native_catalog.rs`. Tool registration is not an effect grant; model output never supplies arbitrary shell/PID or approved file path.
- **Real CI fixes, not skipped tests:** full first matrix found missing Git-test host-executable environment, Windows running a Linux PCI filename test, missing nonfunctional Tauri compile resources, and frontend npm-without-lock handling. Exact-current-branch CI uses the known Lu CI setup and `#[cfg(unix)]` on Linux-specific PCI fixture. Source-locked Windows native/desktop and frontend validation **pending** at ledger update; [run](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42092433718).
- **Full previous matrix at `a6c7153`**: Windows Tauri compile, Linux native, native user-turn dispatch, chat journal, source identity, lockfile graph and diagnostics passed. The Windows native suite failed only on test cleanup of an open file-root handle; `npm run check` used the pre-background string assertion; scanner worker e2e lacked a built worker and had a cwd-dependent duplicated path. Repair commits `8c8f40a` (test/CI code), `5002b0c` (workflow directory), and `db3e557` (exact source manifest) fix those issues without relaxing tool authorization. [Exact candidate Actions run](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/42113888428) was **queued**, not verified, at this ledger update.
- **Important model-path limit:** ordinary desktop `lu_generate` still uses the inference supervisor directly; the separately approved `Ask Reader` path has a supervised model tool turn, but the ZIP/Git Core registrations are not yet wired to that ordinary desktop path. Source/CI success must not be reported as all five tools autonomously operational with a real GGUF.
- **Open acceptance**: complete full platform CI on exact SHA, correct any merge-caused desktop defects, actual real-GGUF model/tool-loop and recovery checks, trusted verified artifact manifests, Reader real files and scanned/OCR support as approved, physical Windows installed NSIS acceptance. Preserve accepted `0.1.0-alpha.2`; **PR #34 is draft, not merged into main**. This is not reason to rebuild any already completed core native tools.

### Prior failures (superseded by later exact-source fixes)

- Original [#24 native-core failure](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/41896488882/job/126807296947) was a test expecting truncation with 300 directories while the actual search ceiling was 512. Fixed and verified on #32; changes merged into dev #24.
- Original [#15 Windows-host failure](https://github.com/vjmichael/Lu-AI-GUI/actions/runs/41895206776/job/126808737558) was stale desktop Cargo.lock. The full Windows Cargo-resolved lockfile was committed and tested on #33; changes merged into dev #15.

## 5. Integration constraints and next handoff

1. **One integration head at a time.** Choose a source-locked integration branch anchored to an exact `main` commit; do not blindly merge all draft branches. Track intersecting files (`src-tauri/lu-core/src/lib.rs`, `core.proto`, desktop `lib.rs`, React `main.jsx`, Cargo manifests and lockfiles).
2. **Batch closely related tool functionality, not redundant implementations.** Verify a coherent tool family with full native checks and desktop dispatch before promoting it. Preserve source-only branches for provenance; safely close superseded PRs **only after** their unique code/evidence is accounted for.
3. **Current technical blockers:** existing Windows worker Job API discrepancy, mixed CI results, absent pinned browser runtime, Reader PDF/OCR limitations and unsynchronized desktop integrations. These are not grounds to declare all tools unbuilt.
4. **Next development gate:** tool branch/source/proto reconciliation → exact-head CI → real GGUF authorized tool loop → Windows installer acceptance; **then** memory integration per the owner decision. The accepted functional alpha remains unchanged.
5. **No faux release:** Update [CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md) only when an owner-accepted milestone/release is established. A source commit or draft PR goes in this ledger, not into released-version notes.

## 6. Change-control workflow (mandatory for future Lu work)

- **Before writing:** read this `main` ledger, [CHANGELOG.md](../CHANGELOG.md), the affected PRs and current **exact head SHAs**; check the target branch and PR base. A conversation summary alone is not the branch status.
- **For each feature PR:** put a compact implementation receipt in the PR description: `LU-ID`, changed paths, original/base/head SHA, source/runtime scope, tests **passed/failed/skipped**, integration and installed-state truth, dependencies and next gate. Use [PR template](../.github/pull_request_template.md). Prefer independent PRs against `main`; use stacked branches only with an explicit dependency and plan to rebase/retarget.
- **When code changes:** update the relevant PR receipt and explicitly state what remains untested. Do **not** record `DONE` merely because a new source file exists or isolated tests pass.
- **At integration/merge:** update this feature map and acceptance/CI evidence on `main` in the same integration pass; add to release changelog **only** after acceptance. Avoid editing the central narrative independently in 20 competing feature branches: keep pending progress in the PR receipt and auto-generated inventory.
- **To refresh PR table:** `node scripts/lu-ledger.mjs --update`; `--check` detects drift. An optional GitHub Actions scheduled refresh updates **only** the marked table, never the manual evidence/status sections. GitHub API metadata cannot certify correctness of code.
- **End of each development session:** report current PR/commit, actual tests, failures, what's now in main, and next concrete gate. This is the handoff to the next conversation.
- **Mandatory group publication (owner directive, 2026-11-18):** after each coherent implementation/repair group, publish its verified exact source and updated receipt to the existing project development branch; confirm the live remote head and source identity before advancing to another group. Native acceptance may remain openly pending; use a source checkpoint without unnecessary CI where appropriate. Preserve existing branches/PRs, batch acceptance and never force-push or merge/release without authorization. A blocked transfer must be recorded with the exact recoverable payload and resolved before further implementation; a local ZIP is recovery evidence, not repository synchronization.

#### CP-04B — bounded provider transport and inert Chromium evidence (2026-11-17)

CP-04A's general Playwright origin routing is insufficient to independently prevent pre-request redirect and DNS rebinding egress. For the **ordinary host-approved search route only**, retain original authenticated-turn grant, native browser custody admission, exact literal human Google URL and owned Node/Chromium pair, but narrow the transport: the fixed worker makes one TLS-validated HTTPS GET (no cookies, auth, body, referrer, redirect follow or retry) to www.google.com. Resolve once, refuse mixed/nonpublic IPv4 answers, pin the validated IPv4 into the connection lookup without resolving again; IPv6-only is unavailable. Enforce response/time/size/type bounds; refuse non-success, throttling, redirects, invalid/incomplete or oversized bodies. No ambient history/file input; model cannot alter the fixed query. This is a bounded provider transport within the owned browser worker, not an unrestricted HTTP tool and not Chromium's normal live-page navigation.

Search profile distinct from general browser profile, included in prior-custody reconciliation on Linux. Both launch paths add search-only DNS resolver denial and sync disable; worker refuses missing exact provider or loopback CDP attachment, takes original context offline, creates a fresh offline context with JavaScript and service workers disabled, denies all routes/WebSockets, parses provider HTML via fixed detached Chromium DOMParser and renders extracted text only. No provider HTML scripts/forms/iframes/assets or result links are attached to live document or opened. Recognize bounded direct or displayed redirect-wrapped HTTPS results as untrusted labels without following them. Reject private literal displayed links and ambiguous wrappers. Detect CAPTCHA/login/consent and no recognized results; terminate this restricted turn instead of attempting human challenge completion in the isolated offline search context. Generic explicit-URL browsing remains unchanged and its redirect/DNS/egress security is NOT established by these search-only tests.

Worker emits SEARCH_OBSERVED (NOT a remote Chromium navigation) only with a genuine 200 transport receipt containing provider body SHA-256, extracted text SHA-256 and bounded count 1..8; Rust requires matching exact URL, successful receipt and identical immutable read, independently hashes the extracted bytes before ledger/index/model synthesis. Persist receipt in conversation observation; citations remain search-results-page only, linked sources not independently verified. No provider body is stored or treated as trusted instruction. Final synthesis and commit remain behind existing citation and queue fences. Public search consent now discloses query, fixed destination and terminal challenge behavior.
